Original range: 790-805
Documentation:

Method code:
790 |   private void validateMinimumChildCount(Node n, int i) {
791 |     boolean valid = false;
792 |     if (i == 1) {
793 |       valid = n.hasChildren();
794 |     } else if (i == 2) {
795 |       valid = n.hasMoreThanOneChild();
796 |     } else {
797 |       valid = n.getChildCount() >= i;
798 |     }
799 | 
800 |     if (!valid) {
801 |       violation(
802 |           "Expected at least " + i + " children, but was "
803 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
804 |     }
805 |   }

===== M0015 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1594-1613
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1594 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1595 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1596 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1597 |     }
1598 | 
1599 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1600 |     Node n, n2;
1601 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1602 |          res == null && n != null;
1603 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1604 |       if (node2 == null) {
1605 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1606 |       }
1607 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1608 |       if (res != null) {
1609 |         return res;
1610 |       }
1611 |     }
1612 |     return res;
1613 |   }

/**
   * Initializes the instance state needed for a compile job if the sources
   * are in modules.

Method code:
328 |   public void initModules(
329 |       List<JSSourceFile> externs, List<JSModule> modules,
330 |       CompilerOptions options) {
331 |     initOptions(options);
332 | 
333 |     checkFirstModule(modules);
334 |     fillEmptyModules(modules);
335 | 
336 |     this.externs = makeCompilerInput(externs, true);
337 |

===== M0020 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java
Original range: 94-109
Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0026 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.cloneTree()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 2077-2091
Documentation:
/**
   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.
   */
Method code:
2077 |   public Node cloneTree() {
2078 |     Node result = cloneNode();
2079 |     for (Node n2 = getFirstChild(); n2 != null; n2 = n2.getNext()) {
2080 |       Node n2clone = n2.cloneTree();
2081 |       n2clone.parent = result;
2082 |       if (result.last != null) {
2083 |         result.last.next = n2clone;
2084 |       }
2085 |       if (result.first == null) {
2086 |         result.first = n2clone;
2087 |       }
2088 |       result.last = n2clone;
2089 |     }
2090 |     return result;
2091 |   }

===== M0034 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ErrorFunctionType.ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ErrorFunctionType.java
Original range: 50-74
Documentation:

Method code:
50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,
53 |         registry.createArrowType(
54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
56 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
57 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE)),
58 |             null),
59 |         null, null, true, true);
60 | 
61 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Errors have the weird behavior in that they can
62 |     // be called as functions, and they will return instances of themselves.
63 |     // Error('x') instanceof Error => true
64 |     //
65 |     // In user-defined types, we would deal with this case by creating
66 |     // a NamedType with the name "Error" and then resolve it later.
67 |     //
68 |     // For native types, we don't really want the native types to
69 |     // depend on type-resolution. So we just set the return type manually
70 |     // at the end of construction.
71 |     //
72 |     // There's similar logic in JSTypeRegistry for Array and RegExp.
73 |     getInternalArrowType().returnType = getInstanceType();
74 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.TypeSafeDispatcher.process(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/TypeSafeDispatcher.java
Original range: 118-282
Documentation:

Method code:
118 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
119 |     switch (node.getType()) {
120 |       case Token.ADD:
121 |       case Token.AND:
122 |       case Token.BITAND:
123 |       case Token.BITOR:
124 |       case Token.BITXOR:
125 |       case Token.COMMA:
126 |       case Token.DIV:
127 |       case Token.EQ:
128 |       case Token.GE:
129 |       case Token.GT:
130 |       case Token.IN:
131 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
132 |       case Token.LE:
133 |       case Token.LSH:
134 |       case Token.LT:
135 |       case Token.MOD:
136 |       case Token.MUL:
137 |       case Token.NE:
138 |       case Token.OR:
139 |       case Token.RSH:
140 |       case Token.SHEQ:
141 |       case Token.SHNE:
142 |       case Token.SUB:
143 |       case Token.URSH:
144 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
145 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
146 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
147 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
148 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
159 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
160 |       case Token.BITNOT:
161 |       case Token.DEC:
162 |       case Token.DELPROP:
163 |       case Token.INC:
164 |       case Token.NEG:
165 |       case Token.NOT:
166 |       case Token.POS:
167 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
168 |       case Token.VOID:
169 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
170 |       case Token.BLOCK:
171 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
172 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
173 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
174 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
175 |         } else {
176 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
177 |                                           node.getClass() +
178 |                                           " type: " +
179 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
180 |         }
181 |       case Token.BREAK:
182 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
183 |       case Token.CALL:
184 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
185 |       case Token.CASE:
186 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
187 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |       case Token.FINALLY:
190 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
191 |       case Token.COLON:
192 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
193 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
194 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
195 |       case Token.DO:
196 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
197 |       case Token.EMPTY:
198 |         return processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node);
199 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
200 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
201 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
202 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
203 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
204 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
205 |         } else {
206 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
207 |                                           node.getClass() +
208 |                                           " type: " +
209 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
210 |         }
211 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
212 |       case Token.FALSE:
213 |       case Token.NULL:
214 |       case Token.THIS:
215 |       case Token.TRUE:
216 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
217 |       case Token.FOR:
218 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
219 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
220 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
221 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
222 |         } else {
223 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
224 |                                           node.getClass() +
225 |                                           " type: " +
226 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
227 |         }
228 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
229 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
230 |       case Token.GETELEM:
231 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
232 |       case Token.GETPROP:
233 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
234 |       case Token.HOOK:
235 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
236 |       case Token.IF:
237 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
238 |       case Token.LABEL:
239 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
240 |       case Token.LP:
241 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
242 |       case Token.NAME:
243 |         return processName((Name) node);
244 |       case Token.NEW:
245 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
246 |       case Token.NUMBER:
247 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
248 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
249 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
250 |       case Token.REGEXP:
251 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
252 |       case Token.RETURN:
253 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
254 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
255 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
256 |       case Token.STRING:
257 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
258 |       case Token.SWITCH:
259 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
260 |       case Token.THROW:
261 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
262 |       case Token.TRY:
263 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
264 |       case Token.CONST:
265 |       case Token.VAR:
266 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
267 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
268 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
269 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
270 |         } else {
271 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
272 |                                           node.getClass() +
273 |                                           " type: " +
274 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
275 |         }
276 |       case Token.WHILE:
277 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
278 |       case Token.WITH:
279 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
280 |     }
281 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
282 |   }

===== M0064 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.toSource(Node,Format,boolean,int,SourceMap,SourceMap.DetailLevel,Charset,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 609-644
Documentation:
/**
   * Converts a tree to js code
   */
Method code:
609 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,
610 |                                  boolean lineBreak,  int lineLengthThreshold,
611 |                                  SourceMap sourceMap,
612 |                                  SourceMap.DetailLevel sourceMapDetailLevel,
613 |                                  Charset outputCharset,
614 |                                  boolean tagAsStrict) {
615 |     Preconditions.checkState(sourceMapDetailLevel != null);
616 | 
617 |     boolean createSourceMap = (sourceMap != null);
618 |     MappedCodePrinter mcp =
619 |         outputFormat == Format.COMPACT
620 |         ? new CompactCodePrinter(
621 |             lineBreak, lineLengthThreshold,
622 |             createSourceMap, sourceMapDetailLevel)
623 |         : new PrettyCodePrinter(
624 |             lineLengthThreshold, createSourceMap, sourceMapDetailLevel);
625 |     CodeGenerator cg =
626 |         outputFormat == Format.TYPED
627 |         ? new TypedCodeGenerator(mcp, outputCharset)
628 |         : new CodeGenerator(mcp, outputCharset);
629 | 
630 |     if (tagAsStrict) {
631 |       cg.tagAsStrict();
632 |     }
633 | 
634 |     cg.add(root);
635 |     mcp.endFile();
636 | 
637 |     String code = mcp.getCode();
638 | 
639 |     if (createSourceMap) {
640 |       mcp.generateSourceMap(sourceMap);
641 |     }
642 | 
643 |     return code;
644 |   }

===== M0086 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 489-509
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
489 |   @Override
490 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
491 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
492 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
493 |     if (unknown) {
494 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
495 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
496 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
497 |         unknown = false;
498 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
499 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
500 |             unknown = true;
501 |             break;
502 |           }
503 |         }
504 |       } else {
505 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
506 |       }
507 |     }
508 |     return unknown;
509 |   }

===== M0090 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 989-991
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
989 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
990 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
991 |   }

===== M0101 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(PrototypeObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 364-407
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
364 |   boolean setPrototype(PrototypeObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
365 |     if (prototype == null) {
366 |       return false;
367 |     }
368 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
369 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
370 |       return false;
371 |     }
372 | 
373 |     PrototypeObjectType oldPrototype = prototypeSlot == null
374 |         ? null : (PrototypeObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
375 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
376 | 
377 |     this.prototypeSlot = new Property("prototype", prototype, true,
378 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
379 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
380 | 
381 |     if (oldPrototype != null) {
382 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
383 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
384 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
385 |     }
386 | 
387 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
388 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
389 |       if (superClass != null) {
390 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
391 |       }
392 | 
393 |       if (isInterface()) {
394 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
395 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
396 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
397 |           }
398 |         }
399 |       }
400 |     }
401 | 
402 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
403 |       clearCachedValues();
404 |     }
405 | 
406 |     return true;
407 |   }

Method code:
647 |   public Node getChildBefore(Node child) {
648 |     if (child == first) {
649 |       return null;
650 |     }

652 |     while (n.next != child) {
653 |       n = n.next;
654 |       if (n == null) {
655 |         throw new RuntimeException("node is not a child");
656 |       }
657 |     }
658 |     return n;
659 |   }

===== M0105 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 470-499
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
470 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
471 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
472 |     int type = n.getType();
473 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
474 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
475 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
476 |     }
477 | 
478 |     curNode = n;
479 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
480 | 
481 |     switch (type) {
482 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
483 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
484 |         break;
485 | 
486 |       default:
487 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
488 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
489 |           // would no longer point to the true next
490 |           Node next = child.getNext();
491 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
492 |           child = next;
493 |         }
494 |         break;
495 |     }
496 | 
497 |     curNode = n;
498 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
499 |   }

163 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
164 |                                    StaticSourceFile sourceFile,

167 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
168 |     IRFactory irFactory = new IRFactory(sourceString, sourceFile,

171 |

184 |

===== M0124 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 457-472
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
457 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
458 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
459 |       Set<String> directives = null;
460 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
461 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
462 |         if (directives == null) {
463 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
464 |         } else {
465 |           directives.add(directive);
466 |         }
467 |       }
468 | 
469 |       if (directives != null) {
470 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
471 |       }
472 |     }

===== M0133 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 429-445
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
429 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
430 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
431 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
432 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
433 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
434 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
435 |         String inputName = input.getName();
436 | 
437 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
438 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
439 |         // will get an error down the line.
440 |         inputs.add(input);
441 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
442 |       }
443 |     }
444 |     return inputs;
445 |   }

===== M0138 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 219-240
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
219 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
220 |     if (isAllType) {
221 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
222 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
223 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
224 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
225 |       } else {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       }
228 |     } else {
229 |       int size = alternates.size();
230 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
231 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
232 |       } else if (size > 1) {
233 |         return null;
234 |       } else if (size == 1) {
235 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
236 |       } else {
237 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
238 |       }
239 |     }
240 |   }

===== M0147 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1034-1147
Documentation:

Method code:
1034 |   private void toString(
1035 |       StringBuilder sb,
1036 |       boolean printSource,
1037 |       boolean printAnnotations,
1038 |       boolean printType) {
1039 |     if (Token.printTrees) {
1040 |       sb.append(Token.name(type));
1041 |       if (this instanceof StringNode) {
1042 |         sb.append(' ');
1043 |         sb.append(getString());
1044 |       } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
1045 |         sb.append(' ');
1046 |         // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
1047 |         // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
1048 |         // toStringTree.
1049 |         if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
1050 |           sb.append("<invalid>");
1051 |         } else {
1052 |           sb.append(first.getString());
1053 |         }
1054 |       } else if (this instanceof ScriptOrFnNode) {
1055 |         ScriptOrFnNode sof = (ScriptOrFnNode) this;
1056 |         if (this instanceof FunctionNode) {
1057 |           FunctionNode fn = (FunctionNode) this;
1058 |           sb.append(' ');
1059 |           sb.append(fn.getFunctionName());
1060 |         }
1061 |         if (printSource) {
1062 |           sb.append(" [source name: ");
1063 |           sb.append(sof.getSourceName());
1064 |           sb.append("] [encoded source length: ");
1065 |           sb.append(sof.getEncodedSourceEnd() - sof.getEncodedSourceStart());
1066 |           sb.append("] [base line: ");
1067 |           sb.append(sof.getBaseLineno());
1068 |           sb.append("] [end line: ");
1069 |           sb.append(sof.getEndLineno());
1070 |           sb.append(']');
1071 |         }
1072 |       } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
1073 |         sb.append(' ');
1074 |         sb.append(getDouble());
1075 |       }
1076 |       if (printSource) {
1077 |         int lineno = getLineno();
1078 |         if (lineno != -1) {
1079 |           sb.append(' ');
1080 |           sb.append(lineno);
1081 |         }
1082 |       }
1083 | 
1084 |       if (printAnnotations) {
1085 |         int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
1086 |         for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
1087 |           int type = keys[i];
1088 |           PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
1089 |           sb.append(" [");
1090 |           sb.append(propToString(type));
1091 |           sb.append(": ");
1092 |           String value;
1093 |           switch (type) {
1094 |             case TARGETBLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it recurses
1095 |               value = "target block property";
1096 |               break;
1097 |             case LOCAL_BLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it is dull
1098 |               value = "last local block";
1099 |               break;
1100 |             case ISNUMBER_PROP:
1101 |               switch (x.getIntValue()) {
1102 |                 case BOTH:
1103 |                   value = "both";
1104 |                   break;
1105 |                 case RIGHT:
1106 |                   value = "right";
1107 |                   break;
1108 |                 case LEFT:
1109 |                   value = "left";
1110 |                   break;
1111 |                 default:
1112 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1113 |               }
1114 |               break;
1115 |             case SPECIALCALL_PROP:
1116 |               switch (x.getIntValue()) {
1117 |                 case SPECIALCALL_EVAL:
1118 |                   value = "eval";
1119 |                   break;
1120 |                 case SPECIALCALL_WITH:
1121 |                   value = "with";
1122 |                   break;
1123 |                 default:
1124 |                   // NON_SPECIALCALL should not be stored
1125 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1126 |               }
1127 |               break;
1128 |             default:
1129 |               value = x.toString();
1130 |               break;
1131 |           }
1132 |           sb.append(value);

===== M0149 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1014-1042
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1014 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1015 |     // unknown
1016 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1017 |       return true;
1018 |     }
1019 |     // equality
1020 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1021 |       return true;
1022 |     }
1023 |     // all type
1024 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1025 |       return true;
1026 |     }
1027 |     // unions
1028 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1029 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1030 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1031 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1032 |           return true;
1033 |         }
1034 |       }
1035 |     }
1036 |     // proxy types
1037 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1038 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1039 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1040 |     }
1041 |     return false;
1042 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)

Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
617 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
618 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
619 |     if (typeSet == null) {
620 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
621 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
622 |     }
623 | 
624 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
625 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
626 | 
627 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
628 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
629 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1332-1340
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1332 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1333 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1334 |       return -1;
1335 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1336 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1337 |     } else {
1338 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1339 |     }
1340 |   }

===== M0170 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-212
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarro sub-lattice. See the commants on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
176 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
177 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
178 |               return this;
179 |             }
180 |           } else {
181 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
182 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
183 |               return this;
184 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
185 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
186 |               it.remove();
187 | 
188 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
189 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
190 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition--;
192 |                 currentIndex--;
193 |               }
194 |             }
195 |           }
196 |           currentIndex++;
197 |         }
198 | 
199 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
200 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
201 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
202 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
203 |         }
204 | 
205 |         alternates.add(alternate);
206 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
207 |       }
208 |     } else {
209 |       result = null;
210 |     }
211 |     return this;
212 |   }

44 |   private Node tryFoldKnownMethods(Node subtree) {
45 |     // For now we only support string methods .join(),
46 |     // .indexOf(), .substring() and .substr()
47 |     // and numeric methods parseInt() and parseFloat().
48 | 
49 |     subtree = tryFoldArrayJoin(subtree);
50 | 
51 |     if (subtree.getType() == Token.CALL) {
52 |       Node callTarget = subtree.getFirstChild();
53 |       if (callTarget == null) {
54 |         return subtree;
55 |       }
56 | 
57 |       if (NodeUtil.isGet(callTarget)) {
58 |         subtree = tryFoldKnownStringMethods(subtree);
59 |       } else {
60 |         subtree = tryFoldKnownNumericMethods(subtree);
61 |       }
62 |     }
63 | 
64 |     return subtree;
65 |   }

===== M0199 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 203-231
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
204 |     maybeEndStatement();
205 | 
206 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
207 |     char prev = getLastChar();
208 | 
209 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
210 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
211 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
212 |       append(" ");
213 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
214 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
215 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
216 |       append(" ");
217 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
218 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     }
221 | 
222 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
223 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
224 | 
225 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
226 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
227 |     // because it looks strange.
228 |     if (binOp) {
229 |       maybeCutLine();
230 |     }
231 |   }

===== M0203 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1156-1294
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1156 |   Node parseInputs() {
1157 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1158 | 
1159 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1160 |     // individual file parse trees.
1161 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1162 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1163 |     }
1164 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1165 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1166 |     }
1167 | 
1168 |     // Parse main js sources.
1169 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1170 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1171 | 
1172 |     externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1173 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1174 | 
1175 |     externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1176 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1177 | 
1178 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1179 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1180 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1181 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1182 |     }
1183 | 
1184 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1185 | 
1186 |     try {
1187 |       // Parse externs sources.
1188 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1189 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1190 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1191 |           return null;
1192 |         }
1193 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1194 |       }
1195 | 
1196 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1197 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1198 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1199 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1200 | 
1201 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1202 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1203 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1204 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1205 |           }
1206 |         }
1207 | 
1208 |         try {
1209 |           inputs =
1210 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1211 |               .manageDependencies(
1212 |                   options.manageClosureDependenciesEntryPoints, inputs);
1213 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1214 |           report(JSError.make(
1215 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1216 |           return null;
1217 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1218 |           report(JSError.make(
1219 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1220 |           return null;
1221 |         }
1222 |       }
1223 | 
1224 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1225 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1226 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1227 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1228 | 
1229 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1230 |         if (n == null) {
1231 |           continue;
1232 |         }
1233 | 
1234 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1235 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1236 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1237 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1238 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1239 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1240 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1241 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1242 | 
1243 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1244 | 
1245 |             externs.add(input);
1246 |             staleInputs = true;
1247 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1248 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1249 |             staleInputs = true;
1250 |           }
1251 |         }
1252 |       }
1253 | 
1254 |       if (staleInputs) {
1255 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1256 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1257 |       }
1258 | 
1259 |       // Build the AST.
1260 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1261 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1262 |         if (n == null) {
1263 |           continue;
1264 |         }
1265 | 
1266 |         if (devMode) {
1267 |           runSanityCheck();
1268 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1269 |             return null;
1270 |           }
1271 |         }
1272 | 
1273 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1274 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1275 | 
1276 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1277 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1278 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1279 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1280 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1281 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1282 |         }
1283 | 
1284 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1285 |       }
1286 | 
1287 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1288 |         return null;
1289 |       }
1290 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1291 |     } finally {
1292 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1293 |     }
1294 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 877-890
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
877 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
878 |     if (item == null) {
879 |       return null;
880 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
881 |       return item.getNext();
882 |     } else {
883 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
884 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
885 |         return item.chain(result);
886 |       } else {
887 |         return item;
888 |       }
889 |     }
890 |   }

===== M0216 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2513-2523
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2513 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2514 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
2515 |     int len = s.length();
2516 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2517 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2518 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2519 |         return false;
2520 |       }
2521 |     }
2522 |     return true;
2523 |   }

===== M0227 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeReplaceKnownMethods.tryFoldKnownStringMethods(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeReplaceKnownMethods.java
Original range: 71-119
Documentation:
/**
   * Try to evaluate known String methods
   *    .indexOf(), .substr(), .substring()
   */
Method code:
71 |   private Node tryFoldKnownStringMethods(Node subtree) {
72 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(subtree.getType() == Token.CALL);
73 | 
74 |     // check if this is a call on a string method
75 |     // then dispatch to specific folding method.
76 |     Node callTarget = subtree.getFirstChild();
77 |     if (callTarget == null) {
78 |       return subtree;
79 |     }
80 | 
81 |     if (!NodeUtil.isGet(callTarget)) {
82 |       return subtree;
83 |     }
84 | 
85 |     Node stringNode = callTarget.getFirstChild();
86 |     Node functionName = stringNode.getNext();
87 | 
88 |     if ((stringNode.getType() != Token.STRING) ||
89 |         (functionName.getType() != Token.STRING)) {
90 |       return subtree;
91 |     }
92 | 
93 |     String functionNameString = functionName.getString();
94 |     Node firstArg = callTarget.getNext();
95 |     if (firstArg == null) {
96 |       if (functionNameString.equals("toLowerCase")) {
97 |         subtree = tryFoldStringToLowerCase(subtree, stringNode);
98 |       } else if (functionNameString.equals("toUpperCase")) {
99 |         subtree = tryFoldStringToUpperCase(subtree, stringNode);
100 |       }
101 |       return subtree;
102 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(firstArg)) {
103 |       if (functionNameString.equals("indexOf") ||
104 |           functionNameString.equals("lastIndexOf")) {
105 |         subtree = tryFoldStringIndexOf(subtree, functionNameString,
106 |             stringNode, firstArg);
107 |       } else if (functionNameString.equals("substr")) {
108 |         subtree = tryFoldStringSubstr(subtree, stringNode, firstArg);
109 |       } else if (functionNameString.equals("substring")) {
110 |         subtree = tryFoldStringSubstring(subtree, stringNode, firstArg);
111 |       } else if (functionNameString.equals("charAt")) {
112 |         subtree = tryFoldStringCharAt(subtree, stringNode, firstArg);
113 |       } else if (functionNameString.equals("charCodeAt")) {
114 |         subtree = tryFoldStringCharCodeAt(subtree, stringNode, firstArg);
115 |       }
116 |     }
117 | 
118 |     return subtree;
119 |   }

===== M0237 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeReplaceKnownMethods.tryFoldArrayJoin(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeReplaceKnownMethods.java
Original range: 367-476
Documentation:
/**
   * Try to fold an array join: ['a', 'b', 'c'].join('') -> 'abc';
   */
Method code:
367 |   private Node tryFoldArrayJoin(Node n) {
368 |     Node callTarget = n.getFirstChild();
369 | 
370 |     if (callTarget == null || !NodeUtil.isGetProp(callTarget)) {
371 |       return n;
372 |     }
373 | 
374 |     Node right = callTarget.getNext();
375 |     if (right != null) {
376 |       if (!NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(right)) {
377 |         return n;
378 |       }
379 |     }
380 | 
381 |     Node arrayNode = callTarget.getFirstChild();
382 |     Node functionName = arrayNode.getNext();
383 | 
384 |     if ((arrayNode.getType() != Token.ARRAYLIT) ||
385 |         !functionName.getString().equals("join")) {
386 |       return n;
387 |     }
388 | 
389 |       // "," is the default, it doesn't need to be explicit
390 | 
391 |     String joinString = (right == null) ? "," : NodeUtil.getStringValue(right);
392 |     List<Node> arrayFoldedChildren = Lists.newLinkedList();
393 |     StringBuilder sb = null;
394 |     int foldedSize = 0;
395 |     Node prev = null;
396 |     Node elem = arrayNode.getFirstChild();
397 |     // Merges adjacent String nodes.
398 |     while (elem != null) {
399 |       if (NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(elem) || elem.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
400 |         if (sb == null) {
401 |           sb = new StringBuilder();
402 |         } else {
403 |           sb.append(joinString);
404 |         }
405 |         sb.append(NodeUtil.getArrayElementStringValue(elem));
406 |       } else {
407 |         if (sb != null) {
408 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(prev);
409 |           // + 2 for the quotes.
410 |           foldedSize += sb.length() + 2;
411 |           arrayFoldedChildren.add(
412 |               Node.newString(sb.toString()).copyInformationFrom(prev));
413 |           sb = null;
414 |         }
415 |         foldedSize += InlineCostEstimator.getCost(elem);
416 |         arrayFoldedChildren.add(elem);
417 |       }
418 |       prev = elem;
419 |       elem = elem.getNext();
420 |     }
421 | 
422 |     if (sb != null) {
423 |       Preconditions.checkNotNull(prev);
424 |       // + 2 for the quotes.
425 |       foldedSize += sb.length() + 2;
426 |       arrayFoldedChildren.add(
427 |           Node.newString(sb.toString()).copyInformationFrom(prev));
428 |     }
429 |     // one for each comma.
430 |     foldedSize += arrayFoldedChildren.size() - 1;
431 | 
432 |     int originalSize = InlineCostEstimator.getCost(n);
433 |     switch (arrayFoldedChildren.size()) {
434 |       case 0:
435 |         Node emptyStringNode = Node.newString("");
436 |         n.getParent().replaceChild(n, emptyStringNode);
437 |         reportCodeChange();
438 |         return emptyStringNode;
439 |       case 1:
440 |         Node foldedStringNode = arrayFoldedChildren.remove(0);
441 |         if (foldedSize > originalSize) {
442 |           return n;
443 |         }
444 |         arrayNode.detachChildren();
445 |         if (foldedStringNode.getType() != Token.STRING) {
446 |           // If the Node is not a string literal, ensure that
447 |           // it is coerced to a string.
448 |           Node replacement = new Node(Token.ADD,
449 |               Node.newString("").copyInformationFrom(n),
450 |               foldedStringNode);
451 |           foldedStringNode = replacement;
452 |         }
453 |         n.getParent().replaceChild(n, foldedStringNode);
454 |         reportCodeChange();
455 |         return foldedStringNode;
456 |       default:
457 |         // No folding could actually be performed.
458 |         if (arrayFoldedChildren.size() == arrayNode.getChildCount()) {
459 |           return n;
460 |         }
461 |         int kJoinOverhead = "[].join()".length();
462 |         foldedSize += kJoinOverhead;
463 |         foldedSize += (right != null) ? InlineCostEstimator.getCost(right) : 0;
464 |         if (foldedSize > originalSize) {
465 |           return n;
466 |         }
467 |         arrayNode.detachChildren();
468 |         for (Node node : arrayFoldedChildren) {
469 |           arrayNode.addChildToBack(node);
470 |         }
471 |         reportCodeChange();
472 |         break;
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     return n;
476 |   }

===== M0238 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.addChangeHandler(CodeChangeHandler)

===== M0250 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 85-735
Documentation:

Method code:
85 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
86 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
87 |       return;
88 |     }
89 | 
90 |     int type = n.getType();
91 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
92 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
93 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
94 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
95 | 
96 |     // Handle all binary operators
97 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
98 |       Preconditions.checkState(
99 |           childCount == 2,
100 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
101 |           opstr, childCount);
102 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
103 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
104 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
105 | 
106 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
107 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
108 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
109 | 
110 |       // Handle associativity.
111 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
112 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
113 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
114 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
115 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
116 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
117 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
118 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
119 |       } else {
120 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
121 |       }
122 |       return;
123 |     }
124 | 
125 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
126 | 
127 |     switch (type) {
128 |       case Token.TRY: {
129 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().getType() == Token.BLOCK &&
130 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
131 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
132 | 
133 |         add("try");
134 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
135 | 
136 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
137 |         // isn't a catch block
138 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
139 |         if (catchblock != null) {
140 |           add(catchblock);
141 |         }
142 | 
143 |         if (childCount == 3) {
144 |           add("finally");
145 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
146 |         }
147 |         break;
148 |       }
149 | 
150 |       case Token.CATCH:
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
152 |         add("catch(");
153 |         add(first);
154 |         add(")");
155 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
156 |         break;
157 | 
158 |       case Token.THROW:
159 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
160 |         add("throw");
161 |         add(first);
162 | 
163 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
164 |         // parse this.
165 |         cc.endStatement(true);
166 |         break;
167 | 
168 |       case Token.RETURN:
169 |         add("return");
170 |         if (childCount == 1) {
171 |           add(first);
172 |         } else {
173 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
174 |         }
175 |         cc.endStatement();
176 |         break;
177 | 
178 |       case Token.VAR:
179 |         if (first != null) {
180 |           add("var ");
181 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
182 |         }
183 |         break;
184 | 
185 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
186 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
187 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
188 |         break;
189 | 
190 |       case Token.NAME:
191 |         if (first == null || first.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
192 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
193 |         } else {
194 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
195 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
196 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
197 |           if (first.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
198 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
199 |           } else {
200 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
201 |             // precedence.
202 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
203 |           }
204 |         }
205 |         break;
206 | 
207 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
208 |         add("[");
209 |         addArrayList(first);
210 |         add("]");
211 |         break;
212 | 
213 |       case Token.LP:
214 |         add("(");
215 |         addList(first);
216 |         add(")");
217 |         break;
218 | 
219 |       case Token.COMMA:
220 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
221 |         addList(first, false, context);
222 |         break;
223 | 
224 |       case Token.NUMBER:
225 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
226 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
227 |         break;
228 | 
229 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
230 |       case Token.VOID:
231 |       case Token.NOT:
232 |       case Token.BITNOT:
233 |       case Token.POS: {
234 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
235 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
236 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
237 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
238 |         break;
239 |       }
240 | 
241 |       case Token.NEG: {
242 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
243 | 
244 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
245 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
246 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
247 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NUMBER) {
248 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
249 |         } else {
250 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
251 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
252 |         }
253 | 
254 |         break;
255 |       }
256 | 
257 |       case Token.HOOK: {
258 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
259 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
260 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
261 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
262 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1);
263 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
264 |         addExpr(last, 1);
265 |         break;
266 |       }
267 | 
268 |       case Token.REGEXP:
269 |         if (first.getType() != Token.STRING ||
270 |             last.getType() != Token.STRING) {
271 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
275 | 
276 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
277 |         if (childCount == 2) {
278 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
279 |         } else {
280 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
281 |           add(regexp);
282 |         }
283 |         break;
284 | 
285 |       case Token.GET_REF:
286 |         add(first);
287 |         break;
288 | 
289 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
290 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
291 |         add(first);
292 |         add(".");
293 |         add((String) n.getProp(Node.NAME_PROP));
294 |         break;
295 | 
296 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
297 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
298 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
299 |         }
300 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
301 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
302 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
303 |           add("(");
304 |         }
305 | 
306 |         add("function");
307 |         add(first);
308 | 
309 |         add(first.getNext());
310 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
311 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
312 | 
313 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
314 |           add(")");
315 |         }
316 |         break;
317 | 
318 |       case Token.GET:
319 |       case Token.SET:
320 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT);
321 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
322 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
323 | 
324 |         // Get methods are unnamed
325 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
326 |         if (type == Token.GET) {
327 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
328 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
329 |           add("get ");
330 |         } else {
331 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
332 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
333 |           add("set ");
334 |         }
335 | 
336 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
337 |         String name = n.getString();
338 |         Node fn = first;
339 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
340 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
341 | 
342 |         // Add the property name.
343 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
344 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
345 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
346 |             // unicode escaped.
347 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
348 |           add(name);
349 |         } else {
350 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
351 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
352 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
353 |             cc.addNumber(d);
354 |           } else {
355 |             addJsString(n.getString());
356 |           }
357 |         }
358 | 
359 |         add(parameters);
360 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
361 |         break;
362 | 
363 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
364 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
365 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
366 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
367 |         }
368 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
369 |         if (preserveBlock) {
370 |           cc.beginBlock();
371 |         }
372 | 
373 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
374 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
375 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
376 |                 !preserveBlock &&
377 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
378 |                 n.getParent().getType() == Token.SCRIPT);
379 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
380 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
381 | 
382 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
383 |           if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
384 |             cc.endStatement();
385 |           }
386 | 
387 |           if (c.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
388 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
389 |           }
390 | 
391 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
392 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
393 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
394 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
395 |           }
396 |         }
397 |         if (preserveBlock) {
398 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
399 |         }
400 |         break;
401 |       }
402 | 
403 |       case Token.FOR:
404 |         if (childCount == 4) {
405 |           add("for(");
406 |           if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
407 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
408 |           } else {
409 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
410 |           }
411 |           add(";");
412 |           add(first.getNext());
413 |           add(";");
414 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
415 |           add(")");
416 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
417 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
418 |         } else {
419 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
420 |           add("for(");
421 |           add(first);
422 |           add("in");
423 |           add(first.getNext());
424 |           add(")");
425 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
426 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
427 |         }
428 |         break;
429 | 
430 |       case Token.DO:
431 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
432 |         add("do");
433 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
434 |         add("while(");
435 |         add(last);
436 |         add(")");
437 |         cc.endStatement();
438 |         break;
439 | 
440 |       case Token.WHILE:
441 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
442 |         add("while(");
443 |         add(first);
444 |         add(")");
445 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
446 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
447 |         break;
448 | 
449 |       case Token.EMPTY:
450 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
451 |         break;
452 | 
453 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
454 |         Preconditions.checkState(
455 |             childCount == 2,
456 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
457 |         Preconditions.checkState(
458 |             last.getType() == Token.STRING,
459 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
460 |         boolean needsParens = (first.getType() == Token.NUMBER);
461 |         if (needsParens) {
462 |           add("(");
463 |         }
464 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
465 |         if (needsParens) {
466 |           add(")");
467 |         }
468 |         add(".");
469 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
470 |         break;
471 |       }
472 | 
473 |       case Token.GETELEM:
474 |         Preconditions.checkState(
475 |             childCount == 2,
476 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
477 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
478 |         add("[");
479 |         add(first.getNext());
480 |         add("]");
481 |         break;
482 | 
483 |       case Token.WITH:
484 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
485 |         add("with(");
486 |         add(first);
487 |         add(")");
488 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
489 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       case Token.INC:
493 |       case Token.DEC: {
494 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
495 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
496 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
497 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
498 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
499 |         if (postProp != 0) {
500 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
501 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
502 |         } else {
503 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
504 |           add(first);
505 |         }
506 |         break;
507 |       }
508 | 
509 |       case Token.CALL:
510 |         // We have two special cases here:
511 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
512 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
513 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
514 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
515 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
516 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
517 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
518 |         // that must be preserved.
519 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
520 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
521 |           add("(0,");
522 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA));
523 |           add(")");
524 |         } else {
525 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
526 |         }
527 |         add("(");
528 |         addList(first.getNext());
529 |         add(")");
530 |         break;
531 | 
532 |       case Token.IF:
533 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
534 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
535 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
536 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
537 |           cc.beginBlock();
538 |         }
539 | 
540 |         add("if(");
541 |         add(first);
542 |         add(")");
543 | 
544 |         if (hasElse) {
545 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
546 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
547 |           add("else");
548 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
549 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
550 |         } else {
551 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
552 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
553 |         }
554 | 
555 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
556 |           cc.endBlock();
557 |         }
558 |         break;
559 | 
560 |       case Token.NULL:
561 |       case Token.THIS:
562 |       case Token.FALSE:
563 |       case Token.TRUE:
564 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
565 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
566 |         break;
567 | 
568 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
569 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
570 |         add("continue");
571 |         if (childCount == 1) {
572 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
573 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
574 |           }
575 |           add(" ");
576 |           add(first);
577 |         }
578 |         cc.endStatement();
579 |         break;
580 | 
581 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
582 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
583 |         add("debugger");
584 |         cc.endStatement();
585 |         break;
586 | 
587 |       case Token.BREAK:
588 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
589 |         add("break");
590 |         if (childCount == 1) {
591 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
592 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
593 |           }
594 |           add(" ");
595 |           add(first);
596 |         }
597 |         cc.endStatement();
598 |         break;
599 | 
600 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
601 |         throw new Error("Unexpected EXPR_VOID. Should be EXPR_RESULT.");
602 | 
603 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
604 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
605 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
606 |         cc.endStatement();
607 |         break;
608 | 
609 |       case Token.NEW:
610 |         add("new ");
611 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
612 | 
613 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
614 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
615 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
616 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(
617 |             first, Token.CALL, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION)) {
618 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
619 |         }
620 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
621 | 
622 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
623 |         Node next = first.getNext();
624 |         if (next != null) {
625 |           add("(");
626 |           addList(next);
627 |           add(")");
628 |         }
629 |         break;
630 | 
631 |       case Token.STRING:
632 |         if (childCount !=
633 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
634 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0)) {
635 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
636 |               "Unexpected String children: " + n.getParent().toStringTree());
637 |         }
638 |         addJsString(n.getString());
639 |         break;
640 | 
641 |       case Token.DELPROP:
642 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
643 |         add("delete ");
644 |         add(first);
645 |         break;
646 | 
647 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
648 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
649 |         if (needsParens) {
650 |           add("(");
651 |         }
652 |         add("{");
653 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
654 |           if (c != first) {
655 |             cc.listSeparator();
656 |           }
657 | 
658 |           if (c.getType() == Token.GET || c.getType() == Token.SET) {
659 |             add(c);
660 |           } else {
661 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.getType() == Token.STRING);
662 |             String key = c.getString();
663 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
664 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
665 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
666 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
667 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
668 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
669 |                 // were unicode escaped.
670 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
671 |               add(key);
672 |             } else {
673 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
674 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
675 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
676 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
677 |               } else {
678 |                 addExpr(c, 1);
679 |               }
680 |             }
681 |             add(":");
682 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1);
683 |           }
684 |         }
685 |         add("}");
686 |         if (needsParens) {
687 |           add(")");
688 |         }
689 |         break;
690 |       }
691 | 
692 |       case Token.SWITCH:
693 |         add("switch(");
694 |         add(first);
695 |         add(")");
696 |         cc.beginBlock();
697 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
698 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
699 |         break;
700 | 
701 |       case Token.CASE:
702 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
703 |         add("case ");
704 |         add(first);
705 |         addCaseBody(last);
706 |         break;
707 | 
708 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
709 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
710 |         add("default");
711 |         addCaseBody(first);
712 |         break;
713 | 
714 |       case Token.LABEL:
715 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
716 |         if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
717 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
718 |         }
719 |         add(first);
720 |         add(":");
721 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
722 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
723 |         break;
724 | 
725 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
726 |       // ignored for our purposes.
727 |       case Token.SETNAME:
728 |         break;
729 | 
730 |       default:
731 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
732 |     }
733 | 
734 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
735 |   }
