Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.RhinoErrorReporter.replacePlaceHolders(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/RhinoErrorReporter.java
Original range: 68-71
Documentation:
/**
   * For each message such as "Not a good use of {0}", replace the place
   * holder {0} with a wild card that matches all possible strings.
   * Also put the any non-place-holder in quotes for regex matching later.
   */
Method code:
68 |   private Pattern replacePlaceHolders(String s) {
69 |     s = Pattern.quote(s);
70 |     return Pattern.compile(s.replaceAll("\\{\\d+\\}", "\\\\E.*\\\\Q"));
71 |   }

===== M0252 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.jsString(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 956-983
Documentation:

Method code:
956 |   String jsString(String s) {
957 |     int singleq = 0, doubleq = 0;
958 | 
959 |     // could count the quotes and pick the optimal quote character
960 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
961 |       switch (s.charAt(i)) {
962 |         case '"': doubleq++; break;
963 |         case '\'': singleq++; break;
964 |       }
965 |     }
966 | 
967 |     String doublequote, singlequote;
968 |     char quote;
969 |     if (singleq < doubleq) {
970 |       // more double quotes so escape the single quotes
971 |       quote = '\'';
972 |       doublequote = "\"";
973 |       singlequote = "\\\'";
974 |     } else {
975 |       // more single quotes so escape the doubles
976 |       quote = '\"';
977 |       doublequote = "\\\"";
978 |       singlequote = "\'";
979 |     }
980 | 
981 |     return strEscape(s, quote, doublequote, singlequote, "\\\\",
982 |         outputCharsetEncoder);
983 |   }

===== M0253 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 631-650
Documentation:

Method code:
631 |   private void addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
632 |       String propertyName, JSType type) {
633 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType && ((ObjectType) type).hasReferenceName()) {
634 |       Map<String, ObjectType> typeSet =
635 |           eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
636 |       if (typeSet == null) {
637 |         typeSet = Maps.newHashMap();
638 |         eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
639 |       }
640 |       ObjectType objType = (ObjectType) type;
641 |       typeSet.put(objType.getReferenceName(), objType);
642 |     } else if (type instanceof NamedType) {
643 |       addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
644 |           propertyName, ((NamedType) type).getReferencedType());
645 |     } else if (type.isUnionType()) {
646 |       for (JSType alternate : type.toMaybeUnionType().getAlternates()) {
647 |         addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, alternate);
648 |       }
649 |     }
650 |   }

===== M0260 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 445-493
Documentation:

Method code:
445 |   @Override
446 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
447 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
448 |       return true;
449 |     }
450 | 
451 |     // Union types
452 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
453 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
454 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
455 |       return false;
456 |     }
457 | 
458 |     // record types
459 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
460 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     // Interfaces
464 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
465 |     // to the interface instance.
466 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
467 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
468 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
469 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
470 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
471 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
472 |           return true;
473 |         }
474 |       }
475 |     }
476 | 
477 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
478 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
479 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
480 |           return true;
481 |         }
482 |       }
483 |     }
484 | 
485 |     // other prototype based objects
486 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
487 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
488 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
489 |       // to avoid guessing.
490 |       return true;
491 |     }
492 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
493 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)

/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1161 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1162 |     switch (type) {
1163 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1164 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1165 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1166 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1167 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1168 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1169 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1170 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1171 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1172 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1173 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1174 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1175 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1176 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1177 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1178 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1179 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1180 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1181 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1182 |       case Token.EQ:
1183 |       case Token.NE:
1184 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1185 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
1186 |       case Token.LT:
1187 |       case Token.GT:
1188 |       case Token.LE:
1189 |       case Token.GE:
1190 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1191 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1192 |       case Token.LSH:
1193 |       case Token.RSH:
1194 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1195 |       case Token.SUB:
1196 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1197 |       case Token.MUL:
1198 |       case Token.MOD:
1199 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1200 |       case Token.INC:
1201 |       case Token.DEC:
1202 |       case Token.NEW:
1203 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1204 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1205 |       case Token.VOID:
1206 |       case Token.NOT:
1207 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1208 |       case Token.POS:
1209 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
1210 | 
1211 |       case Token.CALL:
1212 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1213 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1214 |       // Data values
1215 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1216 |       case Token.EMPTY:  // TODO(johnlenz): remove this.
1217 |       case Token.FALSE:
1218 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1219 |       case Token.NAME:
1220 |       case Token.NULL:
1221 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1222 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
1223 |       case Token.REGEXP:
1224 |       case Token.STRING:
1225 |       case Token.THIS:
1226 |       case Token.TRUE:
1227 |         return 15;
1228 | 
1229 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1230 |                                Node.tokenToName(type) +
1231 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
1232 |     }

===== M0279 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 396-409
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
396 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
397 |       Node ret;
398 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
399 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
400 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
401 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
402 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
403 |       } else {
404 |         ret = transform(n);
405 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
406 |       }
407 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.getType() == Token.STRING);
408 |       return ret;
409 |     }

===== M0282 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.strEscape(String,char,String,String,String,CharsetEncoder)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1005-1075
Documentation:
/** Helper to escape javascript string as well as regular expression */
Method code:
1005 |   static String strEscape(String s, char quote,
1006 |                           String doublequoteEscape,
1007 |                           String singlequoteEscape,
1008 |                           String backslashEscape,
1009 |                           CharsetEncoder outputCharsetEncoder) {
1010 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(s.length() + 2);
1011 |     sb.append(quote);
1012 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1013 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1014 |       switch (c) {
1015 |         case '\0': sb.append("\\x00"); break;
1016 |         case '\n': sb.append("\\n"); break;
1017 |         case '\r': sb.append("\\r"); break;
1018 |         case '\t': sb.append("\\t"); break;
1019 |         case '\\': sb.append(backslashEscape); break;
1020 |         case '\"': sb.append(doublequoteEscape); break;
1021 |         case '\'': sb.append(singlequoteEscape); break;
1022 |         case '>':                       // Break --> into --\> or ]]> into ]]\>
1023 |           if (i >= 2 &&
1024 |               ((s.charAt(i - 1) == '-' && s.charAt(i - 2) == '-') ||
1025 |                (s.charAt(i - 1) == ']' && s.charAt(i - 2) == ']'))) {
1026 |             sb.append("\\>");
1027 |           } else {
1028 |             sb.append(c);
1029 |           }
1030 |           break;
1031 |         case '<':
1032 |           // Break </script into <\/script
1033 |           final String END_SCRIPT = "/script";
1034 | 
1035 |           // Break <!-- into <\!--
1036 |           final String START_COMMENT = "!--";
1037 | 
1038 |           if (s.regionMatches(true, i + 1, END_SCRIPT, 0,
1039 |                               END_SCRIPT.length())) {
1040 |             sb.append("<\\");
1041 |           } else if (s.regionMatches(false, i + 1, START_COMMENT, 0,
1042 |                                      START_COMMENT.length())) {
1043 |             sb.append("<\\");
1044 |           } else {
1045 |             sb.append(c);
1046 |           }
1047 |           break;
1048 |         default:
1049 |           // If we're given an outputCharsetEncoder, then check if the
1050 |           //  character can be represented in this character set.
1051 |           if (outputCharsetEncoder != null) {
1052 |             if (outputCharsetEncoder.canEncode(c)) {
1053 |               sb.append(c);
1054 |             } else {
1055 |               // Unicode-escape the character.
1056 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1057 |             }
1058 |           } else {
1059 |             // No charsetEncoder provided - pass straight latin characters
1060 |             // through, and escape the rest.  Doing the explicit character
1061 |             // check is measurably faster than using the CharsetEncoder.
1062 |             if (c > 0x1f && c < 0x7f) {
1063 |               sb.append(c);
1064 |             } else {
1065 |               // Other characters can be misinterpreted by some js parsers,
1066 |               // or perhaps mangled by proxies along the way,
1067 |               // so we play it safe and unicode escape them.
1068 |               appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1069 |             }
1070 |           }
1071 |       }
1072 |     }
1073 |     sb.append(quote);
1074 |     return sb.toString();
1075 |   }

===== M0293 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 227-290
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
227 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
228 |     this.options = options;
229 |     if (errorManager == null) {
230 |       if (outStream == null) {
231 |         setErrorManager(
232 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
233 |       } else {
234 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
235 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
236 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
237 |         setErrorManager(printer);
238 |       }
239 |     }
240 | 
241 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
242 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
243 |       options.checkTypes = true;
244 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
245 |       options.checkTypes = false;
246 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
247 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
248 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
249 |       // parser type warnings.
250 |       options.setWarningLevel(
251 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
252 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
253 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
257 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
258 |       options.setWarningLevel(
259 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
260 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
261 |     }
262 | 
263 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
264 |       options.setWarningLevel(
265 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
266 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
267 |     }
268 | 
269 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
270 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
271 |     guards.add(
272 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
273 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
274 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
275 | 
276 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
277 | 
278 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
279 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
280 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
281 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
282 |     // error off.
283 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
284 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
285 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
286 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
287 |     }
288 | 
289 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
290 |   }

===== M0296 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getStringValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 177-217
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the value of a node as a String, or null if it cannot be converted.
   * When it returns a non-null String, this method effectively emulates the
   * <code>String()</code> JavaScript cast function.
   */
Method code:
177 |   static String getStringValue(Node n) {
178 |     // TODO(user): regex literals as well.
179 |     switch (n.getType()) {
180 |       case Token.STRING:
181 |         return n.getString();
182 | 
183 |       case Token.NAME:
184 |         String name = n.getString();
185 |         if ("undefined".equals(name)
186 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
187 |             || "NaN".equals(name)) {
188 |           return name;
189 |         }
190 |         break;
191 | 
192 |       case Token.NUMBER:
193 |         return getStringValue(n.getDouble());
194 | 
195 |       case Token.FALSE:
196 |       case Token.TRUE:
197 |       case Token.NULL:
198 |         return Node.tokenToName(n.getType());
199 | 
200 |       case Token.VOID:
201 |         return "undefined";
202 | 
203 |       case Token.NOT:
204 |         TernaryValue child = getPureBooleanValue(n.getFirstChild());
205 |         if (child != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
206 |           return child.toBoolean(true) ? "false" : "true"; // reversed.
207 |         }
208 |         break;
209 | 
210 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
211 |         return arrayToString(n);
212 | 
213 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
214 |         return "[object Object]";
215 |     }
216 |     return null;
217 |   }

197 |   }

Original range: 469-478
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
469 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
470 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
471 |          current != null;
472 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
473 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
474 |         return true;
475 |       }
476 |     }
477 |     return false;
478 |   }

/**
   * When converting arrays to string using Array.prototype.toString or
   * Array.prototype.join, the rules for conversion to String are different
   * than converting each element individually.  Specifically, "null" and
   * "undefined" are converted to an empty string.
   * @param n A node that is a member of an Array.
   * @return The string representation.
   */
Method code:

239 |     return (NodeUtil.isNullOrUndefined(n) || n.getType() == Token.EMPTY)

===== M0345 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1781-1844
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1781 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1782 |     if (type != node.getType()
1783 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1784 |         || getNodeClass(this) != getNodeClass(node)) {
1785 |       return false;
1786 |     }
1787 | 
1788 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1789 |       return false;
1790 |     }
1791 | 
1792 |     if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1793 |       try {
1794 |         int[] indices1 = (int[]) getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1795 |         int[] indices2 = (int[]) node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1796 |         if (indices1 == null) {
1797 |           if (indices2 != null) {
1798 |             return false;
1799 |           }
1800 |         } else if (indices2 == null) {
1801 |           return false;
1802 |         } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1803 |           return false;
1804 |         } else {
1805 |           for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1806 |             if (indices1[i] != indices2[i]) {
1807 |               return false;
1808 |             }
1809 |           }
1810 |         }
1811 |       } catch (Exception e) {
1812 |         return false;
1813 |       }
1814 |     } else if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1815 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1816 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1817 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1818 |         return false;
1819 |       }
1820 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1821 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1822 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1823 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1824 |         return false;
1825 |       }
1826 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1827 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1828 |         return false;
1829 |       }
1830 |     }
1831 | 
1832 |     if (recurse) {
1833 |       Node n, n2;
1834 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1835 |            n != null;
1836 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1837 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1838 |           return false;
1839 |         }
1840 |       }
1841 |     }
1842 | 
1843 |     return true;
1844 |   }

/* OPT there's a noticable delay for the first error!  Maybe it'd
     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties
     * file to avoid (synchronized) text parsing.
     */
Method code:
456 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments)
457 |     {
458 |         final String defaultResource
459 |             = "rhino_ast.java.com.google.javascript.rhino.Messages";
460 | 
461 |         Context cx = Context.getCurrentContext();
462 |         Locale locale = cx != null ? cx.getLocale() : Locale.getDefault();
463 | 
464 |         // ResourceBundle does cacheing.
465 |         ResourceBundle rb = ResourceBundle.getBundle(defaultResource, locale);
466 | 
467 |         String formatString;
468 |         try {
469 |             formatString = rb.getString(messageId);
470 |         } catch (java.util.MissingResourceException mre) {
471 |             throw new RuntimeException

473 |         }

478 |          * single 's.
479 |          */

Documentation:
/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
459 |     @Override
460 |     void maybeCutLine() {
461 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
462 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
463 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
464 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
465 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
466 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
467 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition);
468 |           lineIndex++;
469 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
470 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
471 |         } else {
472 |           startNewLine();
473 |         }
474 |       }
475 |     }

===== M0388 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 233-259
Documentation:

Method code:
233 |   void addNumber(double x) {
234 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
235 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
236 |     char prev = getLastChar();
237 |     if (x < 0 && prev == '-') {
238 |       add(" ");
239 |     }
240 | 
241 |     if ((long) x == x && !isNegativeZero(x)) {
242 |       long value = (long) x;
243 |       long mantissa = value;
244 |       int exp = 0;
245 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
246 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
247 |           mantissa /= 10;
248 |           exp++;
249 |         }
250 |       }
251 |       if (exp > 2) {
252 |         add(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
253 |       } else {
254 |         add(Long.toString(value));
255 |       }
256 |     } else {
257 |       add(String.valueOf(x));
258 |     }
259 |   }

===== M0390 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 330-357
Documentation:

Method code:
330 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
331 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
332 |     // two cases:
333 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
334 |     // and
335 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
336 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
337 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
338 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
339 |     //
340 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
341 |     //
342 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
343 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
344 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
345 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
346 |     //
347 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
348 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
349 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
350 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType() ||
351 |         !(baseType instanceof PrototypeObjectType)) {
352 | 
353 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
354 |           registry, this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
355 |     }
356 |     setPrototype((PrototypeObjectType) baseType, propertyNode);
357 |   }

===== M0393 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeLineBreak()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 433-453
Documentation:

Method code:
433 |     @Override
434 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
435 |       if (lineBreak) {
436 |         if (sawFunction) {
437 |           startNewLine();
438 |           sawFunction = false;
439 |         }
440 |       }
441 | 
442 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
443 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
444 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
445 |       int len = code.length();
446 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
447 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
448 |         if (ch == ';') {
449 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 |       maybeCutLine();
453 |     }

===== M0402 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 485-508
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is an immutable value.
   */
Method code:
485 |   static boolean isImmutableValue(Node n) {
486 |     switch (n.getType()) {
487 |       case Token.STRING:
488 |       case Token.NUMBER:
489 |       case Token.NULL:
490 |       case Token.TRUE:
491 |       case Token.FALSE:
492 |         return true;
493 |       case Token.NOT:
494 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
495 |       case Token.VOID:
496 |       case Token.NEG:
497 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
498 |       case Token.NAME:
499 |         String name = n.getString();
500 |         // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
501 |         // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
502 |         return "undefined".equals(name)
503 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
504 |             || "NaN".equals(name);
505 |     }
506 | 
507 |     return false;
508 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1215 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1216 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1217 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1218 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1219 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1220 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1221 |       } else {
1222 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1223 |       }
1224 |     }
1225 |     return builder.build();
1226 |   }

===== M0415 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PrepareAst.java
Original range: 175-191
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
175 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
176 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.CALL);
177 | 
178 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
179 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
180 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
181 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
182 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
183 |       }
184 | 
185 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
186 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
187 |       if (first.getType() == Token.NAME &&
188 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
189 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
190 |       }
191 |     }

Original range: 235-251
Documentation:

Method code:
235 |   @Override
236 |   boolean defineProperty(String name, JSType type, boolean inferred,
237 |       Node propertyNode) {
238 |     if (hasOwnDeclaredProperty(name)) {
239 |       return false;
240 |     }
241 |     Property newProp = new Property(
242 |         name, type, inferred, propertyNode);
243 |     Property oldProp = properties.get(name);
244 |     if (oldProp != null) {
245 |       // This is to keep previously inferred jsdoc info, e.g., in a
246 |       // replaceScript scenario.
247 |       newProp.setJSDocInfo(oldProp.getJSDocInfo());
248 |     }
249 |     properties.put(name, newProp);
250 |     return true;
251 |   }

   * duplicate inputs.

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

62 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
63 |     Node currentVersionOfNode = n;
64 |     boolean somethingChanged = false;
65 |

Original range: 155-275

Method code:
155 |   public void validateExpression(Node n) {
156 |     switch (n.getType()) {
157 |       // Childless expressions
158 |       case Token.FALSE:
159 |       case Token.NULL:
160 |       case Token.THIS:
161 |       case Token.TRUE:
162 |         validateChildless(n);
163 |         return;
164 | 
165 |       // General uniary ops
166 |       case Token.DELPROP:
167 |       case Token.POS:
168 |       case Token.NEG:
169 |       case Token.NOT:
170 |       case Token.INC:
171 |       case Token.DEC:
172 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
173 |       case Token.VOID:
174 |       case Token.BITNOT:
175 |         validateUnaryOp(n);
176 |         return;
177 | 
178 |       // General binary ops
179 |       case Token.COMMA:
180 |       case Token.OR:
181 |       case Token.AND:
182 |       case Token.BITOR:
183 |       case Token.BITXOR:
184 |       case Token.BITAND:
185 |       case Token.EQ:
186 |       case Token.NE:
187 |       case Token.SHEQ:
188 |       case Token.SHNE:
189 |       case Token.LT:
190 |       case Token.GT:
191 |       case Token.LE:
192 |       case Token.GE:
193 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
194 |       case Token.IN:
195 |       case Token.LSH:
196 |       case Token.RSH:
197 |       case Token.URSH:
198 |       case Token.SUB:
199 |       case Token.ADD:
200 |       case Token.MUL:
201 |       case Token.MOD:
202 |       case Token.DIV:
203 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
204 |         return;
205 | 
206 |       // Assignments
207 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
208 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
209 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
210 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
211 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
212 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
213 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
214 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
215 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
216 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
217 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
218 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
219 |         validateAssignmentExpression(n);
220 |         return;
221 | 
222 |       case Token.HOOK:
223 |         validateTrinaryOp(n);
224 |         return;
225 | 
226 |       // Node types that require special handling
227 |       case Token.STRING:
228 |         validateString(n);
229 |         return;
230 | 
231 |       case Token.NUMBER:
232 |         validateNumber(n);
233 |         return;
234 | 
235 |       case Token.NAME:
236 |         validateName(n);
237 |         return;
238 | 
239 |       case Token.GETELEM:
240 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
241 |         return;
242 | 
243 |       case Token.GETPROP:
244 |         validateGetProp(n);
245 |         return;
246 | 
247 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
248 |         validateArrayLit(n);
249 |         return;
250 | 
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         validateObjectLit(n);
253 |         return;
254 | 
255 |       case Token.REGEXP:
256 |         validateRegExpLit(n);
257 |         return;
258 | 
259 |       case Token.CALL:
260 |         validateCall(n);
261 |         return;
262 | 
263 |       case Token.NEW:
264 |         validateNew(n);
265 |         return;
266 | 
267 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
268 |         validateFunctionExpression(n);
269 |         return;
270 | 
271 |       default:
272 |         violation("Expected expression but was "
273 |             + Node.tokenToName(n.getType()), n);
274 |     }
275 |   }

===== M0538 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 104-148
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
117 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
118 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
119 | 
120 |     if (config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
121 |       // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
123 |       compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(true);
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
127 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
128 |     }
129 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
130 | 
131 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
132 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
133 |     try {
134 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
135 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
136 |       logger.info(
137 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
138 |     } finally {
139 |       Context.exit();
140 |     }
141 |     Node root = null;
142 |     if (astRoot != null) {
143 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
144 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
145 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
146 |     }
147 |     return root;
148 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.toMaybeFunctionType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 280-282
Documentation:
/**
   * Downcasts this to a FunctionType, or returns null if this is not
   * a function.
   *
   * For the purposes of this function, we define a MaybeFunctionType as any
   * type in the sub-lattice
   * { x | LEAST_FUNCTION_TYPE <= x <= GREATEST_FUNCTION_TYPE }
   * This definition excludes bottom types like NoType and NoObjectType.
   *
   * This definition is somewhat arbitrary and axiomatic, but this is the
   * definition that makes the most sense for the most callers.
   */
Method code:
280 |   public FunctionType toMaybeFunctionType() {
281 |     return null;
282 |   }

1440 |   static boolean isAssignmentOp(Node n) {

===== M0560 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1077-1097
Documentation:

Method code:
1077 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1078 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1079 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1080 |       return s;
1081 |     }
1082 | 
1083 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
1084 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1085 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1086 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1087 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1088 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1089 |       // crazy.
1090 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1091 |         sb.append(c);
1092 |       } else {
1093 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1094 |       }
1095 |     }
1096 |     return sb.toString();
1097 |   }

===== M0561 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ComposeWarningsGuard.GuardComparator.compare(WarningsGuard,WarningsGuard)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ComposeWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 60-71
Documentation:

Method code:
60 |     @Override
61 |     public int compare(WarningsGuard a, WarningsGuard b) {
62 |       int priorityDiff = a.getPriority() - b.getPriority();
63 |       if (priorityDiff != 0) {
64 |         return priorityDiff;
65 |       }
66 | 
67 |       // If the warnings guards have the same priority, the one that
68 |       // was added last wins.
69 |       return orderOfAddition.get(b).intValue() -
70 |           orderOfAddition.get(a).intValue();
71 |     }
